<!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "\"\"\"\nAdmin API v1.3.4\n\nSynchronous REST handlers for the React admin control panel.\nWired into webcms.admin.api.create_api() so all /api/v1/admin/* routes\nare registered automatically with the main application.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport uuid\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nfrom webcms.core.request import Request\nfrom webcms.core.response import Response\nfrom webcms.admin.widgets import get_widget_registry, WidgetConfig\n\n\nclass AdminAPI:\n    def __init__(self, db=None, auth=None):\n        self.db = db\n        self.auth = auth\n\n    # ---------------- Database Helpers ----------------\n\n    def _is_kosdb(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Detect if self.db is a KosDB client (dict-like) vs SQLAlchemy session.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return False\n        # KosDB client lacks SQLAlchemy's query attribute\n        return not hasattr(self.db, 'query')\n\n    def _get_model_count(self, model_class, filter_conditions=None) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Get count of model records, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return 0\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            # KosDB: use table name from model, apply simple filters\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            if filter_conditions:\n                # Apply simple equality filters\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    records = [r for r in records if r.get(key) == value]\n            return len(records)\n        else:\n            # SQLAlchemy: use ORM query\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.count()\n\n    def _get_model_list(self, model_class, filter_conditions=None, \n                       order_by=None, limit=None, desc=True) -> list:\n        \"\"\"Get list of model records, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return []\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            # KosDB: get records from table\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    records = [r for r in records if r.get(key) == value]\n            # Sort\n            if order_by:\n                reverse = desc\n                records = sorted(records, \n                               key=lambda x: x.get(order_by) or '', \n                               reverse=reverse)\n            # Limit\n            if limit:\n                records = records[:limit]\n            return records\n        else:\n            # SQLAlchemy ORM\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            if order_by:\n                order_col = getattr(model_class, order_by)\n                if desc:\n                    order_col = order_col.desc()\n                query = query.order_by(order_col)\n            if limit:\n                query = query.limit(limit)\n            return query.all()\n\n    def _get_model_by_id(self, model_class, record_id: str, \n                        id_field='id', extra_filters=None) -> any:\n        \"\"\"Get single record by ID, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return None\n        \n        filters = {id_field: record_id}\n        if extra_filters:\n            filters.update(extra_filters)\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            for r in records:\n                match = True\n                for key, value in filters.items():\n                    if r.get(key) != value:\n                        match = False\n                        break\n                if match:\n                    return r\n            return None\n        else:\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            for key, value in filters.items():\n                query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.first()\n\n    # ---------------- Dashboard ----------------\n\n    def dashboard(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"Return dashboard widgets with real-ish stats.\"\"\"\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.models.content import Post, Page\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n\n        stats = {}\n        if self.db:\n            stats = {\n                \"users\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"active\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False, \"is_active\": True})\n                },\n                \"content\": {\n                    \"posts\": self._get_model_count(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"pages\": self._get_model_count(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                },\n                \"media\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(Media, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                }\n            }\n\n        registry = get_widget_registry()\n        configs = [\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"stats\", title=\"Content Statistics\", type=\"stats\", position=\"main\"),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"activity\", title=\"Recent Activity\", type=\"activity\", position=\"main\", refresh_interval=60),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"health\", title=\"System Health\", type=\"health\", position=\"sidebar\", refresh_interval=30),\n        ]\n        widgets = registry.render_all(self.db, configs) if self.db else [\n            {\"id\": \"stats\", \"title\": \"Content Statistics\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcca\", \"data\": stats},\n            {\"id\": \"activity\", \"title\": \"Recent Activity\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcc5\", \"data\": {\"recent_posts\": 0}},\n            {\"id\": \"health\", \"title\": \"System Health\", \"icon\": \"\u2764\ufe0f\", \"data\": {\"status\": \"ok\"}}\n        ]\n\n        # Ensure every widget has the keys Dashboard.jsx expects\n        for i, widget in enumerate(widgets):\n            widget.setdefault(\"icon\", \"\")\n            widget.setdefault(\"data\", \"\")\n\n        return Response.json({\"widgets\": widgets})\n\n    # ---------------- Content: Pages & Posts ----------------\n\n    def _serialize_page(self, page) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"id\": page.id,\n            \"title\": page.title,\n            \"slug\": page.slug,\n            \"status\": page.status,\n            \"author\": page.author.display_name if page.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": page.updated_at.isoformat() if page.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def _serialize_post(self, post) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"id\": post.id,\n            \"title\": post.title,\n            \"slug\": post.slug,\n            \"status\": post.status,\n            \"author\": post.author.display_name if post.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": post.updated_at.isoformat() if post.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def list_pages(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Page\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"pages\": []})\n        pages = self._get_model_list(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"pages\": [self._serialize_page(p) for p in pages]})\n\n    def create_page(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Page\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.create_page(**data)\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page), 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"updated\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.update_page(page_id, **data)\n            if not page:\n                return Response.not_found()\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page))\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        if manager.delete_page(page_id):\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        return Response.not_found()\n\n    def list_posts(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Post\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"posts\": []})\n        posts = self._get_model_list(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"posts\": [self._serialize_post(p) for p in posts]})\n\n    def create_post(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n\n[-- File truncated: 35273 chars remaining (77.9%). filter_conditions=None) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Get count of model records, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return 0\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            # KosDB: use table name from model, apply simple filters\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            if filter_conditions:\n                # Apply simple equality filters\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    records = [r for r in records if r.get(key) == value]\n            return len(records)\n        else:\n            # SQLAlchemy: use ORM query\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.count()\n\n    def _get_model_list(self, model_class, filter_conditions=None, \n                       order_by=None, limit=None, desc=True) -> list:\n        \"\"\"Get list of model records, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return []\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            # KosDB: get records from table\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    records = [r for r in records if r.get(key) == value]\n            # Sort\n            if order_by:\n                reverse = desc\n                records = sorted(records, \n                               key=lambda x: x.get(order_by) or '', \n                               reverse=reverse)\n            # Limit\n            if limit:\n                records = records[:limit]\n            return records\n        else:\n            # SQLAlchemy ORM\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            if order_by:\n                order_col = getattr(model_class, order_by)\n                if desc:\n                    order_col = order_col.desc()\n                query = query.order_by(order_col)\n            if limit:\n                query = query.limit(limit)\n            return query.all()\n\n    def _get_model_by_id(self, model_class, record_id: str, \n                        id_field='id', extra_filters=None) -> any:\n        \"\"\"Get single record by ID, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return None\n        \n        filters = {id_field: record_id}\n        if extra_filters:\n            filters.update(extra_filters)\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            for r in records:\n                match = True\n                for key, value in filters.items():\n                    if r.get(key) != value:\n                        match = False\n                        break\n                if match:\n                    return r\n            return None\n        else:\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            for key, value in filters.items():\n                query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.first()\n\n    # ---------------- Dashboard ----------------\n\n    def dashboard(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"Return dashboard widgets with real-ish stats.\"\"\"\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.models.content import Post, Page\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n\n        stats = {}\n        if self.db:\n            stats = {\n                \"users\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"active\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False, \"is_active\": True})\n                },\n                \"content\": {\n                    \"posts\": self._get_model_count(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"pages\": self._get_model_count(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                },\n                \"media\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(Media, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                }\n            }\n\n        registry = get_widget_registry()\n        configs = [\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"stats\", title=\"Content Statistics\", type=\"stats\", position=\"main\"),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"activity\", title=\"Recent Activity\", type=\"activity\", position=\"main\", refresh_interval=60),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"health\", title=\"System Health\", type=\"health\", position=\"sidebar\", refresh_interval=30),\n        ]\n        widgets = registry.render_all(self.db, configs) if self.db else [\n            {\"id\": \"stats\", \"title\": \"Content Statistics\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcca\", \"data\": stats},\n            {\"id\": \"activity\", \"title\": \"Recent Activity\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcc5\", \"data\": {\"recent_posts\": 0}},\n            {\"id\": \"health\", \"title\": \"System Health\", \"icon\": \"\u2764\ufe0f\", \"data\": {\"status\": \"ok\"}}\n        ]\n\n        # Ensure every widget has the keys Dashboard.jsx expects\n        for i, widget in enumerate(widgets):\n            widget.setdefault(\"icon\", \"\")\n            widget.setdefault(\"data\", \"\")\n\n        return Response.json({\"widgets\": widgets})\n\n    # ---------------- Content: Pages & Posts ----------------\n\n    def _serialize_page(self, page) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"id\": page.id,\n            \"title\": page.title,\n            \"slug\": page.slug,\n            \"status\": page.status,\n            \"author\": page.author.display_name if page.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": page.updated_at.isoformat() if page.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def _serialize_post(self, post) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"id\": post.id,\n            \"title\": post.title,\n            \"slug\": post.slug,\n            \"status\": post.status,\n            \"author\": post.author.display_name if post.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": post.updated_at.isoformat() if post.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def list_pages(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Page\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"pages\": []})\n        pages = self._get_model_list(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"pages\": [self._serialize_page(p) for p in pages]})\n\n    def create_page(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Page\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.create_page(**data)\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page), 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"updated\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.update_page(page_id, **data)\n            if not page:\n                return Response.not_found()\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page))\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        if manager.delete_page(page_id):\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        return Response.not_found()\n\n    def list_posts(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Post\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"posts\": []})\n        posts = self._get_model_list(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"posts\": [self._serialize_post(p) for p in posts]})\n\n    def create_post(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            post = manager.create_post(**data)\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_post(post), 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_post(self, request: Request, post_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"updated\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            post = manager.update_post(post_id, **data)\n            if not post:\n                return Response.not_found()\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_post(post))\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_post(self, request: Request, post_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        if manager.delete_post(post_id):\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        return Response.not_found()\n\n    # ---------------- Media ----------------\n\n    def list_media(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"media\": []})\n        media = self._get_model_list(Media, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"created_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        result = []\n        for m in media:\n            result.append({\n                \"id\": m.id,\n                \"name\": m.filename,\n                \"filename\": m.filename,\n                \"url\": m.file_url,\n                \"mime_type\": m.mime_type,\n                \"width\": m.width,\n                \"height\": m.height\n            })\n        return Response.json({\"media\": result})\n\n    def upload_media(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.media.manager import MediaManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"uploaded\": True}, 201)\n        manager = MediaManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            # request.files may contain multipart uploads\n            files = getattr(request, \"files\", {}) or {}\n            uploaded = []\n            for name, file_storage in files.items():\n                media = manager.upload(file_storage, uploaded_by=None)\n                uploaded.append({\"id\": media.id, \"name\": media.filename, \"mime_type\": media.mime_type})\n            return Response.json({\"uploaded\": uploaded}, 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_media(self, request: Request, media_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.media.manager import MediaManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": media_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = MediaManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            if manager.delete(media_id):\n                return Response.json({\"id\": media_id, \"deleted\": True})\n            return Response.not_found()\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 500)\n\n    # ---------------- Plugins ----------------\n\n    def list_plugins(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        try:\n            registry = get_registry()\n            plugins = registry.list_available(installed_only=False)\n            result = []\n            for p in plugins:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": p.name,\n                    \"name\": p.name,\n                    \"version\": p.version,\n                    \"description\": p.description,\n                    \"active\": p.active,\n                    \"installed\": p.installed\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"plugins\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"plugins\": []})\n\n    def activate_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.activate(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id, \"active\": success}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    def deactivate_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.deactivate(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id, \"active\": not success}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    def delete_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.uninstall(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Templates & Themes ----------------\n\n    def list_templates(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.engine import TemplateEngine\n        try:\n            engine = TemplateEngine()\n            templates = engine.list_templates()\n            result = []\n            for t in templates:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": t.get(\"id\", t.get(\"name\")),\n                    \"name\": t.get(\"name\"),\n                    \"path\": t.get(\"path\", \"\"),\n                    \"updated_at\": t.get(\"updated_at\", datetime.utcnow().isoformat())\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"templates\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"templates\": []})\n\n    def create_template(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True, \"data\": data}, 201)\n\n    def update_template(self, request: Request, template_id: str) -> Response:\n        data = request.json or {}\n        return Response.json({\"id\": template_id, \"updated\": True, \"data\": data})\n\n    def delete_template(self, request: Request, template_id: str) -> Response:\n        return Response.json({\"id\": template_id, \"deleted\": True})\n\n    def list_themes(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.theme import ThemeManager as TM\n        try:\n            tm = TM()\n            themes = tm.list_themes()\n            result = []\n            for t in themes:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": t.get(\"id\", t.get(\"name\")),\n                    \"name\": t.get(\"name\"),\n                    \"description\": t.get(\"description\", \"\"),\n                    \"active\": t.get(\"active\", False)\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"themes\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"themes\": []})\n\n    def activate_theme(self, request: Request, theme_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.theme import ThemeManager as TM\n        try:\n            tm = TM()\n            tm.activate(theme_id)\n            return Response.json({\"id\": theme_id, \"active\": True})\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": theme_id, \"active\": False, \"error\": str(e)}, 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Users & Roles ----------------\n\n    def list_users(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"users\": []})\n        users = self._get_model_list(User, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"created_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        result = []\n        for u in users:\n            role_name = u.roles[0].name if u.roles else \"user\"\n            result.append({\n                \"id\": u.id,\n                \"username\": u.username,\n                \"email\": u.email,\n                \"display_name\": u.display_name,\n                \"role\": role_name,\n                \"is_active\": u.is_active,\n                \"roles\": [r.name for r in u.roles]\n            })\n        return Response.json({\"users\": result})\n\n    def create_user(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.auth.password import PasswordHasher\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n\n        try:\n            hasher = PasswordHasher()\n            user = User(\n                username=data.get(\"username\"),\n                email=data.get(\"email\"),\n                password_hash=hasher.hash(data.get(\"password\", \"changeme\")),\n                display_name=data.get(\"display_name\", data.get(\"username\")),\n                is_active=data.get(\"is_active\", True)\n            )\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                # KosDB: append to users list\n                users = self.db.get('users', [])\n                user.id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n                users.append(user.__dict__ if hasattr(user, '__dict__') else user)\n                self.db['users'] = users\n            else:\n                # SQLAlchemy\n                self.db.add(user)\n                self.db.commit()\n            return Response.json({\n                \"id\": user.id if hasattr(user, 'id') else user.get('id'),\n                \"username\": user.username if hasattr(user, 'username') else user.get('username'),\n                \"email\": user.email if hasattr(user, 'email') else user.get('email'),\n                \"role\": \"user\",\n                \"is_active\": user.is_active if hasattr(user, 'is_active') else settings = self.db.query(Setting).all()\n                for s in settings:\n                    defaults[s.key] = self._coerce_setting(s.value, s.type)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        return Response.json({\"settings\": defaults})\n\n    def update_settings(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.system import Setting\n        data = request.json or settings.append({\n                            'key': key,\n                            'value': str(value),\n                            'type': self._guess_type(value)\n                        })\n     return value.lower() in (\"true\", \"1\", \"yes\", \"on\")\n        if type_ == \"int\":\n            try:\n                return int(value)\n            except ValueError:\n <!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "\"\"\"\nAdmin API v1.3.4\n\nSynchronous REST handlers for the React admin control panel.\nWired into webcms.admin.api.create_api() so all /api/v1/admin/* routes\nare registered automatically with the main application.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport uuid\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nfrom webcms.core.request import Request\nfrom webcms.core.response import Response\nfrom webcms.admin.widgets import get_widget_registry, WidgetConfig\n\n\nclass AdminAPI:\n    def __init__(self, db=None, auth=None):\n        self.db = db\n        self.auth = auth\n\n    # ---------------- Database Helpers ----------------\n\n    def _is_kosdb(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Detect if self.db is a KosDB client (dict-like) vs SQLAlchemy session.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return False\n        # KosDB client lacks SQLAlchemy's query attribute\n        return not hasattr(self.db, 'query')\n\n    def _get_model_count(self, model_class, filter_conditions=None) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Get count of model records, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return 0\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            # KosDB: use table name from model, apply simple filters\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            if filter_conditions:\n                # Apply simple equality filters\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    records = [r for r in records if r.get(key) == value]\n            return len(records)\n        else:\n            # SQLAlchemy: use ORM query\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.count()\n\n    def _get_model_list(self, model_class, filter_conditions=None, \n                       order_by=None, limit=None, desc=True) -> list:\n        \"\"\"Get list of model records, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return []\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            # KosDB: get records from table\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    records = [r for r in records if r.get(key) == value]\n            # Sort\n            if order_by:\n                reverse = desc\n                records = sorted(records, \n                               key=lambda x: x.get(order_by) or '', \n                               reverse=reverse)\n            # Limit\n            if limit:\n                records = records[:limit]\n            return records\n        else:\n            # SQLAlchemy ORM\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            if order_by:\n                order_col = getattr(model_class, order_by)\n                if desc:\n                    order_col = order_col.desc()\n                query = query.order_by(order_col)\n            if limit:\n                query = query.limit(limit)\n            return query.all()\n\n    def _get_model_by_id(self, model_class, record_id: str, \n                        id_field='id', extra_filters=None) -> any:\n        \"\"\"Get single record by ID, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return None\n        \n        filters = {id_field: record_id}\n        if extra_filters:\n            filters.update(extra_filters)\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            for r in records:\n                match = True\n                for key, value in filters.items():\n                    if r.get(key) != value:\n                        match = False\n                        break\n                if match:\n                    return r\n            return None\n        else:\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            for key, value in filters.items():\n                query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.first()\n\n    # ---------------- Dashboard ----------------\n\n    def dashboard(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"Return dashboard widgets with real-ish stats.\"\"\"\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.models.content import Post, Page\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n\n\n[-- File truncated: 42907 chars remaining (89.6%). limit=None, desc=True) -> list:\n        \"\"\"Get list of model records, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return []\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            # KosDB: get records from table\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', for r in records if r.get(key) == value]\n            # Sort\n            if order_by:\n                reverse = desc\n                records = sorted(records, \n                               key=lambda x: x.get(order_by) or '', \n                               reverse=reverse)\n            # Limit\n            if limit:\n                records = records[:limit]\n            return records\n        else:\n            # SQLAlchemy ORM\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            if order_by:\n                order_col = getattr(model_class, order_by)\n                if desc:\n                    order_col = order_col.desc()\n                query = query.order_by(order_col)\n            if limit:\n                query = query.limit(limit)\n            return query.all()\n\n    def _get_model_by_id(self, model_class, record_id: str, \n                        id_field='id', extra_filters=None) -> any:\n        \"\"\"Get single record by ID, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return None\n        \n        filters = {id_field: record_id}\n        if extra_filters:\n            filters.update(extra_filters)\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            for r in records:\n                match = True\n                for key, value in filters.items():\n                    if r.get(key) != value:\n                        match = False\n                        break\n                if match:\n                    return r\n            return None\n        else:\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            for key, value in filters.items():\n                query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.first()\n\n    # ---------------- Dashboard ----------------\n\n    def dashboard(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"Return dashboard widgets with real-ish stats.\"\"\"\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.models.content import Post, Page\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n\n        stats = {}\n        if self.db:\n            stats = {\n                \"users\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"active\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False, \"is_active\": True})\n                },\n                \"content\": {\n                    \"posts\": self._get_model_count(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"pages\": self._get_model_count(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                },\n                \"media\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(Media, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                }\n            }\n\n        registry = get_widget_registry()\n        configs = [\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"stats\", title=\"Content Statistics\", type=\"stats\", position=\"main\"),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"activity\", title=\"Recent Activity\", type=\"activity\", position=\"main\", refresh_interval=60),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"health\", title=\"System Health\", type=\"health\", position=\"sidebar\", refresh_interval=30),\n        ]\n        widgets = registry.render_all(self.db, configs) if self.db else [\n            {\"id\": \"stats\", \"title\": \"Content Statistics\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcca\", \"data\": stats},\n            {\"id\": \"activity\", \"title\": \"Recent Activity\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcc5\", \"data\": {\"recent_posts\": 0}},\n            {\"id\": \"health\", \"title\": \"System Health\", \"icon\": \"\u2764\ufe0f\", \"data\": {\"status\": \"ok\"}}\n        ]\n\n        # Ensure every widget has the keys Dashboard.jsx expects\n        for i, widget in enumerate(widgets):\n            widget.setdefault(\"icon\", \"\")\n            widget.setdefault(\"data\", \"\")\n\n        return Response.json({\"widgets\": widgets})\n\n    # ---------------- Content: Pages & Posts ----------------\n\n    def _serialize_page(self, page) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"id\": page.id,\n            \"title\": page.title,\n            \"slug\": page.slug,\n            \"status\": page.status,\n            \"author\": page.author.display_name if page.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": page.updated_at.isoformat() if page.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def _serialize_post(self, post) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"id\": post.id,\n            \"title\": post.title,\n            \"slug\": post.slug,\n            \"status\": post.status,\n            \"author\": post.author.display_name if post.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": post.updated_at.isoformat() if post.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def list_pages(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Page\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"pages\": []})\n        pages = self._get_model_list(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"pages\": [self._serialize_page(p) for p in pages]})\n\n    def create_page(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Page\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.create_page(**data)\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page), 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n \"\"\"Detect if self.db is a KosDB client (dict-like) vs SQLAlchemy session.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return False\n        # KosDB client lacks SQLAlchemy's query attribute\n        return not hasattr(self.db, 'query')\n\n    def _get_model_count(self, model_class, filter_conditions=None) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Get count of model records, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return 0\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            # KosDB: use table name from model, apply simple filters\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            if filter_conditions:\n                # Apply simple equality filters\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    records = [r for r in records if r.get(key) == value]\n            return len(records)\n        else:\n            # SQLAlchemy: use ORM query\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.count()\n\n    def _get_model_list(self, model_class, filter_conditions=None, \n                       order_by=None, limit=None, desc=True) -> list:\n        \"\"\"Get list of model records, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return []\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            # KosDB: get records from table\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    records = [r for r in records if r.get(key) == value]\n            # Sort\n            if order_by:\n                reverse = desc\n                records = sorted(records, \n                               key=lambda x: x.get(order_by) or '', \n                               reverse=reverse)\n            # Limit\n            if limit:\n                records = records[:limit]\n            return records\n        else:\n            # SQLAlchemy ORM\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            if order_by:\n                order_col = getattr(model_class, order_by)\n                if desc:\n                    order_col = order_col.desc()\n                query = query.order_by(order_col)\n            if limit:\n                query = query.limit(limit)\n            return query.all()\n\n    def _get_model_by_id(self, model_class, record_id: str, \n                        id_field='id', extra_filters=None) -> any:\n        \"\"\"Get single record by ID, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return None\n        \n        filters = {id_field: record_id}\n        if extra_filters:\n            filters.update(extra_filters)\n        \n        if self._is_kosdb():\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            records = self.db.get(table_name, [])\n            for r in records:\n                match = True\n                for key, value in filters.items():\n                    if r.get(key) != value:\n                        match = False\n                        break\n                if match:\n                    return r\n            return None\n        else:\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            for key, value in filters.items():\n                query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.first()\n\n    # ---------------- Dashboard ----------------\n\n    def dashboard(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"Return dashboard widgets with real-ish stats.\"\"\"\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.models.content import Post, Page\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n\n        stats = {}\n        if self.db:\n            stats = {\n                \"users\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"active\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False, \"is_active\": True})\n                },\n                \"content\": {\n                    \"posts\": self._get_model_count(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"pages\": self._get_model_count(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                },\n                \"media\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(Media, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                }\n            }\n\n        registry = get_widget_registry()\n        configs = [\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"stats\", title=\"Content Statistics\", type=\"stats\", position=\"main\"),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"activity\", title=\"Recent Activity\", type=\"activity\", position=\"main\", refresh_interval=60),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"health\", title=\"System Health\", type=\"health\", position=\"sidebar\", refresh_interval=30),\n        ]\n        widgets = registry.render_all(self.db, configs) if self.db else [\n            {\"id\": \"stats\", \"title\": \"Content Statistics\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcca\", \"data\": stats},\n            {\"id\": \"activity\", \"title\": \"Recent Activity\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcc5\", \"data\": {\"recent_posts\": 0}},\n            {\"id\": \"health\", \"title\": \"System Health\", \"icon\": \"\u2764\ufe0f\", \"data\": {\"status\": \"ok\"}}\n        ]\n\n        # Ensure every widget has the keys Dashboard.jsx expects\n        for i, widget in enumerate(widgets):\n            widget.setdefault(\"icon\", \"\")\n            widget.setdefault(\"data\", \"\")\n\n        return Response.json({\"widgets\": widgets})\n\n    # ---------------- Content: Pages & Posts ----------------\n\n    def _serialize_page(self, page) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"id\": page.id,\n            \"title\": page.title,\n            \"slug\": page.slug,\n            \"status\": page.status,\n            \"author\": page.author.display_name if page.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": page.updated_at.isoformat() if page.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def _serialize_post(self, post) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"id\": post.id,\n            \"title\": post.title,\n            \"slug\": post.slug,\n            \"status\": post.status,\n            \"author\": post.author.display_name if post.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": post.updated_at.isoformat() if post.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def list_pages(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Page\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"pages\": []})\n        pages = self._get_model_list(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"pages\": [self._serialize_page(p) for p in pages]})\n\n    def create_page(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Page\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.create_page(**data)\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page), 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"updated\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.update_page(page_id, **data)\n            if not page:\n                return Response.not_found()\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page))\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        if manager.delete_page(page_id):\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        return Response.not_found()\n\n    def list_posts(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Post\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"posts\": []})\n        posts = self._get_model_list(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"posts\": [self._serialize_post(p) for p in posts]})\n\n    def create_post(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            post = manager.create_post(**data)\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_post(post), 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_post(self, request: Request, post_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"updated\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            post = manager.update_post(post_id, **data)\n            if not post:\n                return Response.not_found()\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_post(post))\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_post(self, request: Request, post_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        if manager.delete_post(post_id):\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        return Response.not_found()\n\n    # ---------------- Media ----------------\n\n    def list_media(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"media\": []})\n        media = self._get_model_list(Media, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"created_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        result = []\n        for m in media:\n            # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n            if isinstance(m, dict):\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": m.get('id'),\n                    \"name\": m.get('filename'),\n                    \"filename\": m.get('filename'),\n                    \"url\": m.get('file_url'),\n                    \"mime_type\": m.get('mime_type'),\n                    \"width\": m.get('width'),\n                    \"height\": m.get('height')\n                })\n            else:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": m.id,\n                    \"name\": m.filename,\n                    \"filename\": m.filename,\n                    \"url\": m.file_url,\n                    \"mime_type\": m.mime_type,\n                    \"width\": m.width,\n                    \"height\": m.height\n                })\n        return Response.json({\"media\": result})\n\n    def upload_media(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.media.manager import MediaManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"uploaded\": True}, 201)\n        manager = MediaManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            # request.files may contain multipart uploads\n            files = getattr(request, \"files\", {}) or {}\n            uploaded = []\n            for name, file_storage in files.items():\n                media = manager.upload(file_storage, uploaded_by=None)\n                uploaded.append({\"id\": media.id, \"name\": media.filename, \"mime_type\": media.mime_type})\n            return Response.json({\"uploaded\": uploaded}, 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_media(self, request: Request, media_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.media.manager import MediaManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": media_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = MediaManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            if manager.delete(media_id):\n                return Response.json({\"id\": media_id, \"deleted\": True})\n            return Response.not_found()\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 500)\n\n    # ---------------- Plugins ----------------\n\n    def list_plugins(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        try:\n            registry = get_registry()\n            plugins = registry.list_available(installed_only=False)\n            result = []\n            for p in plugins:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": p.name,\n                    \"name\": p.name,\n                    \"version\": p.version,\n                    \"description\": p.description,\n                    \"active\": p.active,\n                    \"installed\": p.installed\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"plugins\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"plugins\": []})\n\n    def activate_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.activate(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id, \"active\": success}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    def deactivate_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.deactivate(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id, \"active\": not success}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    def delete_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.uninstall(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Templates & Themes ----------------\n\n    def list_templates(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.engine import TemplateEngine\n        try:\n            engine = TemplateEngine()\n            templates = engine.list_templates()\n            result = []\n            for t in templates:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": t.get(\"id\", t.get(\"name\")),\n                    \"name\": t.get(\"name\"),\n                    \"path\": t.get(\"path\", \"\"),\n                    \"updated_at\": t.get(\"updated_at\", datetime.utcnow().isoformat())\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"templates\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"templates\": []})\n\n    def create_template(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True, \"data\": data}, 201)\n\n    def update_template(self, request: Request, template_id: str) -> Response:\n        data = request.json or {}\n        return Response.json({\"id\": template_id, \"updated\": True, \"data\": data})\n\n    def delete_template(self, request: Request, template_id: str) -> Response:\n        return Response.json({\"id\": template_id, \"deleted\": True})\n\n    def list_themes(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.theme import ThemeManager as TM\n        try:\n            tm = TM()\n            themes = tm.list_themes()\n            result = []\n            for t in themes:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": t.get(\"id\", t.get(\"name\")),\n                    \"name\": t.get(\"name\"),\n                    \"description\": t.get(\"description\", \"\"),\n                    \"active\": t.get(\"active\", False)\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"themes\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"themes\": []})\n\n    def activate_theme(self, request: Request, theme_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.theme import ThemeManager as TM\n        try:\n            tm = TM()\n            tm.activate(theme_id)\n            return Response.json({\"id\": theme_id, \"active\": True})\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": theme_id, \"active\": False, \"error\": str(e)}, 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Users & Roles ----------------\n\n    def list_users(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"users\": []})\n        users = self._get_model_list(User, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"created_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        result = []\n        for u in users:\n            role_name = u.roles[0].name if u.roles else \"user\"\n            result.append({\n                \"id\": u.id,\n                \"username\": u.username,\n                \"email\": u.email,\n                \"display_name\": u.display_name,\n                \"role\": role_name,\n                \"is_active\": u.is_active,\n                \"roles\": [r.name for r in u.roles]\n            })\n        return Response.json({\"users\": result})\n\n    def create_user(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.auth.password import PasswordHasher\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n\n        try:\n            hasher = PasswordHasher()\n            user = User(\n                username=data.get(\"username\"),\n                email=data.get(\"email\"),\n                password_hash=hasher.hash(data.get(\"password\", \"changeme\")),\n                display_name=data.get(\"display_name\", data.get(\"username\")),\n                is_active=data.get(\"is_active\", True)\n            )\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                # KosDB: append to users list\n                users = self.db.get('users', [])\n                user.id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n                users.append(user.__dict__ if hasattr(user, '__dict__') else user)\n                self.db['users'] = users\n            else:\n                # SQLAlchemy\n                self.db.add(user)\n                self.db.commit()\n            return Response.json({\n                \"id\": user.id if hasattr(user, 'id') else user.get('id'),\n                \"username\": user.username if hasattr(user, 'username') else user.get('username'),\n                \"email\": user.email if hasattr(user, 'email') else user.get('email'),\n                \"role\": \"user\",\n                \"is_active\": user.is_active if hasattr(user, 'is_active') else user.get('is_active')\n            }, 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_user(self, request: Request, user_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": user_id, \"updated\": True})\n        user = self._get_model_by_id(User, user_id, extra_filters={\"is_deleted\": False})\n        if not user:\n            return Response.not_found()\n        try:\n            if \"username\" in data:\n                user.username = data[\"username\"]\n            if \"email\" in data:\n                user.email = data[\"email\"]\n            if \"display_name\" in data:\n                user.display_name = data[\"display_name\"]\n            if \"is_active\" in data:\n                user.is_active = bool(data[\"is_active\"])\n            if \"password\" in data and data[\"password\"]:\n                from webcms.auth.password import PasswordHasher\n                user.password_hash = PasswordHasher().hash(data[\"password\"])\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.commit()\n            return Response.json({\n                \"id\": user.id,\n                \"username\": user.username,\n                \"email\": user.email,\n                \"role\": user.roles[0].name if user.roles else \"user\",\n                \"is_active\": user.is_active\n            })\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_user(self, request: Request, user_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": user_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        user = self._get_model_by_id(User, user_id, extra_filters={\"is_deleted\": False})\n        if not user:\n            return Response.not_found()\n        user.is_deleted = True\n        if not self._is_kosdb():\n            self.db.commit()\n        return Response.json({\"id\": user_id, \"deleted\": True})\n\n    def list_roles(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import Role\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"roles\": []})\n        roles = self._get_model_list(Role, order_by=\"name\")\n        result = []\n        for r in roles:\n            # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n            if isinstance(r, dict):\n                perms = r.get('permissions', '')\n                perms_list = [p.strip() for p in perms.split(',')] if perms else []\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": r.get('id'),\n                    \"name\": r.get('name'),\n                    \"description\": r.get('description'),\n                    \"permissions\": perms_list\n                })\n            else:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": r.id,\n                    \"name\": r.name,\n                    \"description\": r.description,\n                    \"permissions\": r.permissions_list\n                })\n        return Response.json({\"roles\": result})\n\n    def update_role(self, request: Request, role_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import Role\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": role_id, \"updated\": True})\n        role = self._get_model_by_id(Role, role_id)\n        if not role:\n            return Response.not_found()\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                # KosDB: update dict directly\n                if \"permissions\" in data:\n                    role['permissions'] = \",\".join(data[\"permissions\"])\n                if \"description\" in data:\n                    role['description'] = data[\"description\"]\n                # Save back to db\n                roles = self.db.get('roles', [])\n                for i, r in enumerate(roles):\n                    if r.get('id') == role_id:\n                        roles[i] = role\n                        break\n                self.db['roles'] = roles\n                perms = role.get('permissions', '')\n                perms_list = [p.strip() for p in perms.split(',')] if perms else []\n                return Response.json({\n                    \"id\": role.get('id'),\n                    \"name\": role.get('name'),\n                    \"description\": role.get('description'),\n                    \"permissions\": perms_list\n                })\n            else:\n                # SQLAlchemy\n                if \"permissions\" in data:\n                    role.permissions = \",\".join(data[\"permissions\"])\n                if \"description\" in data:\n                    role.description = data[\"description\"]\n                self.db.commit()\n                return Response.json({\n                    \"id\": role.id,\n                    \"name\": role.name,\n                    \"description\": role.description,\n                    \"permissions\": role.permissions_list\n                })\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Settings ----------------\n\n    def get_settings(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.system import Setting\n        defaults = {\n            \"site_name\": \"WebCMS\",\n            \"site_url\": \"https://example.com\",\n            \"admin_email\": \"admin@example.com\",\n            \"default_language\": \"en\",\n            \"posts_per_page\": 10,\n            \"cache_enabled\": True,\n            \"cache_ttl\": 300,\n            \"search_enabled\": True,\n            \"elasticsearch_url\": \"http://localhost:9200\",\n            \"notifications_enabled\": True,\n            \"smtp_host\": \"localhost\",\n            \"smtp_port\": 587,\n            \"smtp_user\": \"\",\n            \"smtp_pass\": \"\",\n            \"csp_enabled\": True,\n            \"require_https\": False\n        }\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"settings\": defaults})\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                settings = self.db.get('settings', [])\n                for s in settings:\n                    defaults[s.get('key')] = self._coerce_setting(s.get('value'), s.get('type'))\n            else:\n                settings = self.db.query(Setting).all()\n                for s in settings:\n                    defaults[s.key] = self._coerce_setting(s.value, s.type)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        return Response.json({\"settings\": defaults})\n\n    def update_settings(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.system import Setting\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"updated\": True, \"settings\": data})\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                settings = self.db.get('settings', [])\n                for key, value in data.items():\n                    existing = next((s for s in settings if s.get('key') == key), None)\n                    if existing:\n                        existing['value'] = str(value)\n                        existing['type'] = self._guess_type(value)\n                    else:\n                        settings.append({\n                            'key': key,\n                            'value': str(value),\n                            'type': self._guess_type(value)\n                        })\n                self.db['settings'] = settings\n            else:\n                for key, value in data.items():\n                    existing = self.db.query(Setting).filter(Setting.key == key).first()\n                    if existing:\n                        existing.value = str(value)\n                        existing.type = self._guess_type(value)\n                    else:\n                        setting = Setting(key=key, value=str(value), type=self._guess_type(value))\n                        self.db.add(setting)\n                self.db.commit()\n            return Response.json({\"updated\": True, \"settings\": data})\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _guess_type(value):\n        if isinstance(value, bool):\n            return \"bool\"\n        if isinstance(value, int):\n            return \"int\"\n        if isinstance(value, float):\n            return \"float\"\n        return \"str\"\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _coerce_setting(value, type_):\n        if type_ == \"bool\":\n            return value.lower() in (\"true\", \"1\", \"yes\", \"on\")\n        if type_ == Router\nfrom .middleware import MiddlewareStack\nfrom .container import Container\n\n\nclass Application:\n    \"\"\"Main WebCMS application class.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, config_path: Optional[str] = None):\n        self.root_path = Path(__file__).parent.parent.absolute()\n        self.config = self._load_config(config_path)\n        self.container = Container()\n        self.router = Router()\n        self.middleware = MiddlewareStack()\n        self.plugins: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.hooks: Dict[str, List[Callable]] = {}\n        \n        self._setup_logging()\n        self._init_container()\n        \n        logging.info(f\"WebCMS {self._get_version()} initialized\")\n    \n    def _load_config(self, config_path: Optional[str]) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Load configuration from YAML file.\"\"\"\n        if config_path is None:\n            config_path = self.root_path / \"config\" / \"config.yaml\"\n        \n        default_config = {\n            \"app\": {\n                \"name\": \"WebCMS\",\n                \"debug\": False,\n                \"secret_key\": \"change-me-in-production\",\n                \"timezone\": \"UTC\"\n            },\n            \"server\": {\n                \"host\": \"0.0.0.0\",\n                \"port\": 8000,\n                \"workers\": 4\n            },\n            \"database\": {\n                \"url\": \"sqlite:///webcms.db\",\n                \"pool_size\": 10\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if os.path.exists(config_path):\n            with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n                user_config = yaml.safe_load(f)\n                self._deep_update(default_config, user_config)\n        \n        return default_config\n    \n    def _deep_update(self, base: Dict, update: Dict) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Recursively update nested dictionaries.\"\"\"\n        for key, value in update.items():\n            if key in base and isinstance(base[key], dict) and isinstance(value, dict):\n                self._deep_update(base[key], value)\n            else:\n                base[key] = value\n    \n    def _setup_logging(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Configure logging.\"\"\"\n        log_format = \"%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s\"\n        logging.basicConfig(\n            level=logging.DEBUG if self.config[\"app\"][\"debug\"] else logging.INFO,\n            format=log_format\n        )\n        self.logger = logging.getLogger(\"webcms\")\n    \n    def _init_container(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Initialize dependency injection container.\"\"\"\n        self.container.register(\"app\", self)\n        self.container.register(\"config\", self.config)\n        self.container.register(\"router\", self.router)\n        self.container.register(\"hooks\", self.hooks)\n    \n    def route(self, path: str, methods: Optional[List[str]] = None):\n        \"\"\"Decorator to register a route.\"\"\"\n        def decorator(func: Callable) -> Callable:\n            self.router.add(path, func, methods or [\"GET\"])\n            return func\n        return decorator\n    \n    def use(self, middleware) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Register middleware.\"\"\"\n        self.middleware.add(middleware)\n    \n    def wsgi_app(self, environ: Dict, start_response: Callable) -> List[bytes]:\n        \"\"\"WSGI application entry point.\"\"\"\n        request = Request.from_wsgi(environ)\n        response = self.middleware.process(request, self._handle_request)\n        return response.to_wsgi(start_response)\n    \n    def _handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"Handle request and return response.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            handler, params = self.router.match(request.path, request.method)\n            \n            if handler is None:\n                return Response.not_found()\n            \n            response = handler(request, **params)\n            \n            if not isinstance(response, Response):\n                response = Response(response)\n            \n            return response\n            \n        except Exception as e:\n            self.logger.exception(\"Request handling error\")\n            return Response.error(\"Internal Server Error\", 500)\n    \n    def run(self, host: Optional[str] = None, port: Optional[int] = None, debug: bool = False, **kwargs) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Run development server.\"\"\"\n        from wsgiref.simple_server import make_server\n        \n        host = host or self.config[\"server\"][\"host\"]\n        port = port or self.config[\"server\"][\"port\"]\n        \n        if debug:\n            self.config[\"app\"][\"debug\"] = True\n            self._setup_logging()\n        \n        server = make_server(host, port, self.wsgi_app)\n        self.logger.info(f\"Server running on http://{host}:{port}\")\n        \n        try:\n            server.serve_forever()\n        except KeyboardInterrupt:\n            self.logger.info(\"Shutting down...\")\n    \n    def _get_version(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Get WebCMS version.\"\"\"\n WSGI.\"\"\"\n        return self.wsgi_app(environ, start_response)", "sessionId": 333, "rowId": 14, "timestamp": 1784277015.7857265, "date": "2026-07-17", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

<!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "\"\"\"\nWebCMS Middleware System\n\nRequest/response processing pipeline.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Callable, List, Optional\nfrom .request import Request\nfrom .response import Response\n\n\nclass MiddlewareStack:\n    \"\"\"Middleware execution stack.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self.middlewares: List[Callable] = []\n    \n    def add(self, middleware: Callable) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Add middleware to stack.\"\"\"\n        self.middlewares.append(middleware)\n    \n    def process(\n        self,\n        request: Request,\n        handler: Callable[[Request], Response]\n    ) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"\n        Process request through middleware chain.\n        \n        Args:\n            request: HTTP request\n            handler: Final request handler\n        \n        Returns:\n            HTTP response\n        \"\"\"\n        # Build chain from inside out\n        wrapped = handler\n        for middleware in reversed(self.middlewares):\n            wrapped = self._wrap(middleware, wrapped)\n        \n        return wrapped(request)\n    \n    def _wrap(\n        self,\n        middleware: Callable,\n        next_handler: Callable[[Request], Response]\n    ) -> Callable[[Request], Response]:\n        \"\"\"Wrap handler with middleware.\"\"\"\n        def wrapper(request: Request) -> Response:\n            return middleware(request, next_handler)\n        return wrapper\n\n\nclass CommonMiddleware:\n    \"\"\"Common middleware for all requests.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, app=None):\n        self.app = app\n    \n    def __call__(\n        self,\n        request: Request,\n        handler: Callable[[Request], Response]\n    ) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"Process request.\"\"\"\n        # Add common headers\n        response = handler(request)\n        response.headers.setdefault(\"X-Content-Type-Options\", \"nosniff\")\n        response.headers.setdefault(\"X-Frame-Options\", \"DENY\")\n        response.headers.setdefault(\"X-XSS-Protection\", \"1; mode=block\")\n        return response\n\n\nclass CORSMiddleware:\n    \"\"\"CORS handling middleware.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        allow_origins: List[str] = None,\n        allow_methods: List[str] = None,\n        allow_headers: List[str] = None\n    ):\n        self.allow_origins = allow_origins or [\"*\"]\n        self.allow_methods = allow_methods or [\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"DELETE\", \"OPTIONS\"]\n        self.allow_headers = allow_headers or [\"Content-Type\", \"Authorization\"]\n    \n    def __call__(\n        self,\n        request: Request,\n        handler: Callable[[Request], Response]\n    ) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"Handle CORS.\"\"\"\n        if request.method == \"OPTIONS\":\n            # Preflight request\n            return Response(\n                \"\",\n                200,\n                headers={\n                    \"Access-Control-Allow-Origin\": \", \".join(self.allow_origins),\n                    \"Access-Control-Allow-Methods\": \", \".join(self.allow_methods),\n                    \"Access-Control-Allow-Headers\": \", \".join(self.allow_headers)\n                }\n            )\n        \n        response = handler(request)\n        response.headers[\"Access-Control-Allow-Origin\"] = self.allow_origins[0]\n        return response", "sessionId": 333, "rowId": 14, "timestamp": 1784277019.1081834, "date": "2026-07-17", "name": "ReadFile"} -->
